- **sensitivity-analysis**: 灵敏度分析引擎（统计学博士审校版）。先按题型自适应分发（优化/预测/评价/机理/仿真），再选方法工具箱：单因素扫描、Morris筛选、Sobol全局（含收敛诊断）、蒙特卡洛不确定性传播（BCa CI）。内置竞赛模板：优化类（华数杯实测）、评价类、事件研究类。关键约束：每个方法含假设检验和局限性声明，产出 SENSITIVITY_REPORT.md。 ([Crayonnan/dsh-math-modeling-skills-Gatecraft-](https://github.com/Crayonnan/dsh-math-modeling-skills-Gatecraft-/tree/HEAD/skills/sensitivity-analysis) · ★9)
- **sensitivity-analysis**: 关键假设变动对结果的敏感性测试，识别关键驱动因素。触发词：敏感性分析、Sensitivity Analysis、敏感性、假设、测试、驱动。当用户需要敏感性分析相关分析时使用。 ([haiting202-web/jiufeng-invest](https://github.com/haiting202-web/jiufeng-invest/tree/HEAD/agent-preset/ai-finance/skills/sensitivity-analysis) · ★9 · 2 copies)
- **sensitivity-analysis**: This skill guides testing the robustness of conclusions. ([jg-you/bayesian-workflow](https://github.com/jg-you/bayesian-workflow/tree/HEAD/skills/sensitivity-analysis) · ★4)
- **sensitivity-analysis**: Measure how much each input drives a model or decision output, varying inputs over stated ranges and ranking their influence before trusting the result. ([SamoTech/skills-tree](https://github.com/SamoTech/skills-tree/tree/HEAD/agent-skills/sensitivity-analysis) · ★4)
- **sensitivity-analysis**: Runs global sensitivity and uncertainty analysis to rank assumptions and parameters that drive results. ([tatashbar/Economic-Modeling-Studio](https://github.com/tatashbar/Economic-Modeling-Studio/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/sensitivity-analysis) · ★4 · 3 copies)
- **sensitivity-analysis**: 灵敏度分析：参数扰动、单因素/多因素分析、Monte Carlo 模拟、龙卷风图/蛛网图。触发词: 灵敏度分析、参数敏感性、sensitivity analysis、Monte Carlo、鲁棒性测试、参数扰动。 ([Best6668/AMIS](https://github.com/Best6668/AMIS/tree/HEAD/skills/sensitivity-analysis) · ★2)
- **sensitivity-analysis**: Tests conclusion robustness by perturbing parameters and observing rank changes. Shared across scoring, portfolio, and steel-manning campaigns. ([yogsoth-ai/convergence](https://github.com/yogsoth-ai/convergence/tree/HEAD/skills/sensitivity-analysis) · ★2)
- **sensitivity-analysis**: 油藏模拟参数敏感性分析：系统评估各参数对模拟结果的影响程度，为历史拟合和方案优化提供指导。 ([Adair-Shuai/QwenPaw](https://github.com/Adair-Shuai/QwenPaw/tree/HEAD/plugins/bundle/ugsci/skills/sensitivity-analysis) · 2 copies)
- **sensitivity-analysis**: Banking-grade sensitivity, Monte Carlo, scenario, and break-even analysis for project finance. Use when an engagement needs tornado diagrams, P80 NPV, DSCR distributions, or stress testing for bankability discussions. ([HossamDaoud83/Omega_Plugins_Official](https://github.com/HossamDaoud83/Omega_Plugins_Official/tree/HEAD/plugins/finance/skills/sensitivity-analysis))
- **sensitivity-analysis**: Use to stress-test a financial model — vary the key drivers and find what breaks viability. Triggers: "run sensitivity", "what if churn doubles", "how robust is this", "which assumption matters most", G1/G3 financial passes. ([jakubsielewicz/au-sme-compliance](https://github.com/jakubsielewicz/au-sme-compliance/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/sensitivity-analysis) · 2 copies)
- **sensitivity-analysis**: Use this skill after multiverse-analysis and before self-review. Identifies which parameters and assumptions, if changed within plausible ranges, would move the answer enough to change the decision. Differs from multiverse — multiverse varies analytical *paths*; sensitivity varies *parameters within a fixed path*. Trigger when results are about to be summarized for decision-making, especially when assumptions are non-obvious (discount rates, lookback windows, attribution windows, threshold cutoffs, smoothing parameters). ([samwedll/analytics-skills](https://github.com/samwedll/analytics-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/analytics-skills/skills/sensitivity-analysis))
